				lifeMax = 6;

			defDamage = damage;
			defDefense = defense;
			defLifeMax = lifeMax;
		}
	}

	public void ScaleStats_ByDifficulty()

			value = (int)(value * GameDifficultyData.EnemyMoneyDropMultiplier.Sample(difficulty));

104	function attackSlot(e: Enemy, table: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]>): { atk: number; inSlot: number; dur: number } {

180	  const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
181	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
182	  e.vx = (dx / d) * spd;

185	  e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx);
186	  const s = Math.sign(dx) || st.dir;
187	  st.dir = s;

189	  st.spr = -s;

193	function dukeHover(e: Enemy, player: Player, acc: number, spd: number, sideOff: number, st: DukeX) {
194	  if (e.ai1 === 0) e.ai1 = sideOff * Math.sign(e.cx - player.cx || 1);

198	  e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (dx / d) * spd, acc);
199	  e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (dy / d) * spd, acc);
200	  const s = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || st.dir;
201	  if (e.ai2 === 0 && s !== st.dir) e.spin += Math.PI;

203	  if (st.spr !== -st.dir) e.spin += Math.PI;
204	  st.spr = -st.dir;
205	}

246	  if (st.spr === 1) target += Math.PI;
247	  if (target < 0) target += TAU; else if (target > TAU) target -= TAU;
248	  if (e.ai0 === -1 || e.ai0 === 3 || e.ai0 === 4 || e.ai0 === 8) target = 0; // :49271-49286
249	  let step = 0.04;                                                            // num23 :49287
250	  if (e.ai0 === 1 || e.ai0 === 6 || e.ai0 === 7) step = 0;                    // :49288-49295
251	  if (e.ai0 === 3 || e.ai0 === 4 || e.ai0 === 8) step = 0.01;                 // :49296-49307
252	  let rot = e.spin;
253	  if (rot < target) rot += (target - rot > Math.PI ? -step : step);
254	  if (rot > target) rot += (rot - target > Math.PI ? step : -step);
255	  if (rot > target - step && rot < target + step) rot = target;
256	  if (rot < 0) rot += TAU; else if (rot > TAU) rot -= TAU;
257	  if (rot > target - step && rot < target + step) rot = target;
258	  e.spin = rot;
259	
260	  // ---- 透明度(:49346-49363;近似为 spawnAlpha 渐变)----
261	  // 入实心块 +15/出块 −15,钳 [0,150](★上限 150 非 255——入块半隐,2026-08-19 台账 E 修)
262	  if (e.ai0 !== -1 && e.ai0 < 9) e.spawnAlpha = inSolid(game, e) ? Math.min(150, e.spawnAlpha + 15) : Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 15);
263	
264	  let invuln = false; // flag7(dontTakeDamage = !flag7,:50126)
265	  switch (e.ai0) {
266	    case -1: { // 出生(:49365-49414)
267	      invuln = true;
268	      e.vx *= 0.98; e.vy *= 0.98;
269	      const s = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || st.dir;
270	      st.dir = s; st.spr = -s;
271	      if (e.ai2 > 20) {
272	        e.vy = -2;
273	        e.spawnAlpha = clamp(e.spawnAlpha - 5 + (inSolid(game, e) ? 15 : 0), 0, 150); // :49375-49391
274	      } else if (e.spawnAlpha < 255) e.spawnAlpha = 255;
275	      if (e.ai2 === DUKE.nadoT - 30) { // :49392 出生尘环
276	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8FD8FF', 18, 2.4, { life: 40 });
277	        game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :49404 PlaySound(29,…,20) 满响(曾借 roar 0.8)
278	      }
279	      e.ai2 += 1;
280	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.spawnT) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
281	      break;
282	    }
283	    case 0: { // 一阶段悬浮(:49415-49543)
284	      if (player.dead) break;
285	      dukeHover(e, player, hoverAcc, hoverSpd, 300, st);
286	      e.ai2 += 1;
287	      if (e.ai2 >= hoverT) {
288	        const next = dukePick1(e, enraged);
289	        if (next === 1) dukeCharge(e, player, chargeSpd, 1, st);
290	        else if (next === 2) { e.ai0 = 2; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
291	        else if (next === 3) {
292	          e.ai0 = 3; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
293	          if (enraged) e.ai2 = DUKE.nadoT - 40; // :49530-49533 狂暴跳过前摇
294	        } else if (next === 4) { e.ai0 = 4; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
295	      }
296	      break;
297	    }
298	    case 1: { // 冲撞(:49544-49566)
299	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx + e.w / 2 - Math.cos(e.spin) * e.w * 0.3, e.cy + e.h / 2 - Math.sin(e.spin) * e.h * 0.3, '#8FD8FF', 2, 1.5, { life: 18 });
300	      e.ai2 += 1;
301	      if (e.ai2 >= chargeT) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 2; }
302	      break;
303	    }
304	    case 2: { // 泡泡弹幕(:49567-49637)
305	      if (player.dead) break;
306	      if (e.ai1 === 0) e.ai1 = 300 * Math.sign(e.cx - player.cx || 1);
307	      const dx = player.cx + e.ai1 - e.cx - e.vx;
308	      const dy = player.cy - 200 - e.cy - e.vy;
309	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
310	      e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (dx / d) * DUKE.bubbleSpd, DUKE.bubbleAcc);
311	      e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (dy / d) * DUKE.bubbleSpd, DUKE.bubbleAcc);
312	      if (e.ai2 === 0) game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :49608 PlaySound(29,…,20) 满响(曾借 roar 0.5)
313	      if (e.ai2 % DUKE.bubbleEvery === 0) { // :49610-49618 每 4t 吐一颗爆裂泡泡(NPC 371)
314	        const ddx = player.cx - e.cx, ddy = player.cy - e.cy;
315	        const dd = Math.hypot(ddx, ddy) || 1;
316	        const bub = spawnPart(game, e, 371);
317	        if (bub) {
318	          bub.x = e.cx + (ddx / dd) * ((e.w + 20) / 2) - bub.w / 2;
319	          bub.y = e.cy + (ddy / dd) * ((e.w + 20) / 2) + 45 - bub.h / 2;
320	        }
321	        // :49612 每吐一颗播 PlaySound(4,…,19)=NPC_Killed_19(case4=死音库)
322	        game.playSfxWav?.('NPC_Killed_19', 1, e.cx, e.cy);
323	      }
324	      const s = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || st.dir;
325	      st.dir = s; st.spr = -s;
326	      e.ai2 += 1;
327	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.bubbleT) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
328	      break;
329	    }
330	    case 3: dukeSharknado(e, game, player, st, false); break;          // :49638-49660
331	    case 4: { // 一→二阶段变身(:49661-49679)
332	      invuln = true;
333	      e.vx *= 0.98;
334	      e.vy = lerp(e.vy, 0, 0.02);
335	      if (e.ai2 === DUKE.p1T - 60) game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :49668 PlaySound(29,…,20)(曾借 roar 0.9)
336	      e.ai2 += 1;
337	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.p1T) { e.ai0 = 5; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
338	      break;
339	    }
340	    case 5: { // 二阶段悬浮(:49680-49811)
341	      if (player.dead) break;
342	      dukeHover(e, player, hoverAcc, hoverSpd, 300, st);
343	      e.ai2 += 1;
344	      if (e.ai2 >= hoverT) {
345	        const next = dukePick2(e, enraged);
346	        if (next === 1) dukeCharge(e, player, chargeSpd, 6, st);
347	        else if (next === 2) { // 旋转泡泡冲刺起手(:49782-49797):20 速直冲
348	          const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
349	          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
350	          e.vx = (dx / d) * DUKE.spinDashSpd;
351	          e.vy = (dy / d) * DUKE.spinDashSpd;
352	          e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx);
353	          const s = Math.sign(dx) || st.dir;
354	          st.dir = s;
355	          if (st.spr === 1) e.spin += Math.PI;
356	          st.spr = -s;
357	          e.ai0 = 7; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
358	        } else if (next === 3) { e.ai0 = 8; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
359	        else if (next === 4) { e.ai0 = 9; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; } // expert
360	      }
361	      break;
362	    }
363	    case 6: { // 二阶段冲撞(:49812-49834)
364	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8FD8FF', 2, 1.5, { life: 18 });
365	      e.ai2 += 1;
366	      if (e.ai2 >= chargeT) { e.ai0 = 5; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 2; }
367	      break;
368	    }
369	    case 7: { // 旋转泡泡冲刺(:49835-49864)
370	      if (e.ai2 === 0) game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :49839 PlaySound(29,…,20)(曾借 roar 0.6)
371	      if (e.ai2 % DUKE.spinEvery === 0) { // 每 4t 沿垂直方向甩一颗泡泡
372	        const vlen = Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) || 1;
373	        const bub = spawnPart(game, e, 371);
374	        game.playSfxWav?.('NPC_Killed_19', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :49843 每颗吐音(case4,19)
375	        if (bub) {
376	          bub.bInit = true; bub.ai0 = 0; bub.ai1 = 0;          // 预置初速,跳过自初始化
377	          bub.ai3 = (80 + Math.random() * 41) / 100;            // :49751 随机 scale
378	          bub.x = e.cx + (e.vx / vlen) * ((e.w + 20) / 2) - bub.w / 2;
379	          bub.y = e.cy + (e.vy / vlen) * ((e.w + 20) / 2) + 45 - bub.h / 2;
380	          const ang = Math.atan2(e.vy / vlen, e.vx / vlen) + (Math.PI / 2) * st.dir;
381	          bub.vx = Math.cos(ang) * DUKE.spinBubbleSpd;
382	          bub.vy = Math.sin(ang) * DUKE.spinBubbleSpd;
383	        }
384	      }
385	      // 速度/身体同步旋转(-num20*direction,:49854-49855)
386	      {
387	        const r = -DUKE_SPIN_RATE * st.dir;
388	        const cos = Math.cos(r), sin = Math.sin(r);
389	        const nvx = e.vx * cos - e.vy * sin, nvy = e.vx * sin + e.vy * cos;
390	        e.vx = nvx; e.vy = nvy;
391	        e.spin -= DUKE_SPIN_RATE * st.dir;
392	      }
393	      e.ai2 += 1;
394	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.spinT) { e.ai0 = 5; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
395	      break;
396	    }
397	    case 8: dukeSharknado(e, game, player, st, true); break;           // :49865-49885
398	    case 9: { // expert 二段狂暴变身(:49886-49931;经典不可达,保留供 expert 接线)
399	      invuln = true;
400	      if (e.ai2 < DUKE.p2T - 90) e.spawnAlpha = clamp(e.spawnAlpha + (inSolid(game, e) ? 15 : -15), 0, 255);
401	      else e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(255, e.spawnAlpha + 4);
402	      e.vx *= 0.98;
403	      e.vy = lerp(e.vy, 0, 0.02);
404	      if (e.ai2 === DUKE.p2T - 60) game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :49920 PlaySound(29,…,20)(曾借 roar 0.9)
405	      e.ai2 += 1;
406	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.p2T) { e.ai0 = 10; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
407	      break;
408	    }
409	    case 10: { // expert 狂暴段悬浮(:49932-50018)
410	      if (player.dead) break;
411	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(255, e.spawnAlpha + 25); // 隐身逼近
412	      dukeHover(e, player, hoverAcc, hoverSpd, 360, st);
413	      e.ai2 += 1;
414	      if (e.ai2 >= hoverT) {
415	        const a3 = Math.floor(e.ai3);
416	        const next = (a3 === 0 || a3 === 2 || a3 === 3 || a3 === 5 || a3 === 6 || a3 === 7) ? 1 : 2;
417	        if (next === 1) dukeCharge(e, player, chargeSpd, 11, st);
418	        else { e.ai0 = 12; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
419	      }
420	      break;
421	    }
422	    case 11: { // expert 狂暴段冲撞(:50019-50047)
423	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 25);
424	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8FD8FF', 2, 1.5, { life: 18 });
425	      e.ai2 += 1;
426	      if (e.ai2 >= chargeT) { e.ai0 = 10; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; }
427	      break;
428	    }
429	    case 12: { // expert 瞬移爆冲(:50048-50107)
430	      invuln = true;
431	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(255, e.spawnAlpha + 17);
432	      e.vx *= 0.98;
433	      e.vy = lerp(e.vy, 0, 0.02);
434	      if (e.ai2 === DUKE.teleT / 2) { // :50066-50093 中点瞬移到玩家另一侧
435	        if (e.ai1 === 0) e.ai1 = 300 * Math.sign(e.cx - player.cx || 1);
436	        e.x = player.cx - e.ai1 - e.w / 2;
437	        e.y = player.cy - 200 - e.h / 2;
438	        // :50075-50091 侧翻补偿:瞬移后 num41=玩家在新身哪侧,direction 重定;
439	        // spriteDirection != -direction 时 rotation += π(朝向翻转 180°,
440	        // 2026-08-19 台账 E 补——曾漏 = 瞬移后鱼头朝向不变);(原版 :50078 的
441	        // ai[2]==0 门在 ai[2]==num15/2 内恒假=死代码,略)
442	        const s = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx);
443	        if (s !== 0) {
444	          st.dir = s;
445	          if (st.spr !== -s) e.spin += Math.PI;
446	          st.spr = -s;
447	        }
448	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8FD8FF', 14, 2.4, { life: 30 });
449	        game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :50064 PlaySound(29,…,20)(曾借 roar 0.6)
450	      }
451	      e.ai2 += 1;
452	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.teleT) {
453	        e.ai0 = 10; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 1;
454	        if (e.ai3 >= 9) e.ai3 = 0;
455	      }
456	      break;
457	    }
458	    case 13: { // expert 狂暴段旋转冲刺(:50108-50125;不吐泡泡)
459	      if (e.ai2 === 0) game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :50112 PlaySound(29,…,20)(曾借 roar 0.6)
460	      {
461	        const r = -DUKE_SPIN_RATE * st.dir;
462	        const cos = Math.cos(r), sin = Math.sin(r);
463	        const nvx = e.vx * cos - e.vy * sin, nvy = e.vx * sin + e.vy * cos;
464	        e.vx = nvx; e.vy = nvy;
465	        e.spin -= DUKE_SPIN_RATE * st.dir;
466	      }
467	      e.ai2 += 1;
468	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.spinT) { e.ai0 = 10; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; }
469	      break;
470	    }
471	    default: break;
472	  }
473	  if (invuln) e.iframes = 2;
474	  e.facing = st.spr;
475	  e.x += e.vx;
476	  e.y += e.vy;
477	}
478	
479	/** 鲨鱼龙卷(状态 3 :49638-49660 / 状态 8 :49865-49885 共用):
480	 *  减速悬停,ai2==60(nadoT-30)时发射龙卷水弹 proj 385(伤害 0 的落地生成器)——
481	 *  小龙卷两发斜落(±dir*2, 8)、克苏龙卷一发原地(0,0,ai0=1=追踪型,ai2=狂暴旗)。
482	 *  落地/近身 50px 后由弹体自身 Kill 生成 384/386 龙卷,龙卷逐段向上生长并周期
483	 *  NewNPC 372/373 鲨鱼(见 DukeSharknadoBolt / DukeSharknadoTornado)。 */
484	function dukeSharknado(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player, st: DukeX, big: boolean) {
485	  e.vx *= 0.98;
486	  e.vy = lerp(e.vy, 0, 0.02);
487	  if (e.ai2 === DUKE.nadoT - 30) {
488	    if (big) {
489	      // :49871 大龙卷 = PlaySound(29,…,20) Zombie_20 满响(曾借 roar 0.7)
490	      game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);
491	      // 状态 8:克苏龙卷弹(:49873-49876;ai2=flag6 狂暴→追踪速度 +12)
492	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoBolt(e.cx, e.cy, 0, 0, true, dukeEnraged(game, player), e));
493	    } else {
494	      // :49644 小龙卷 = PlaySound(29,…,9) Zombie_9 满响
495	      game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_9', 1, e.cx, e.cy);
496	      // 状态 3:左右两发斜落(:49646-49651;vector8 = rotation·(dir,0)·(w+20)/2 偏移)
497	      const ox = Math.cos(e.spin) * st.dir * (e.w + 20) / 2;
498	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoBolt(e.cx + ox, e.cy, st.dir * 2, 8, false, false, e));
499	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoBolt(e.cx + ox, e.cy, -st.dir * 2, 8, false, false, e));
500	    }
501	  }
502	  e.ai2 += 1;
503	  if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.nadoT) { e.ai0 = big ? 5 : 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
504	}
505	
506	/** 投射桶入桶辅助(同 bossAI.addProj;类在本文件内,避免跨模块构造注入) */
507	function addProj(game: GameHooks, p: Entity): void {
508	  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: Entity, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(p, 'projectiles');
509	}
510	
511	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
512	// 龙卷水弹 385(Projectile.cs:28402-28482 AI / :70714-70778 Kill 落龙卷)
513	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
514	// aiStyle 65:ai1==0(状态 3 小弹)→ vy 余弦摆动斜落,撞块/入水/超时 Kill;
515	//  ai1>0(状态 8 克苏弹)→ 追踪玩家,速 4(+12 狂暴)+age/20,贴身 50px Kill。
516	//  Kill:小弹原地落 384 鲨鱼龙卷(伤 40);大弹自弹体 tile 向下扫描 100 格落 386
517	//  克苏龙卷(伤 80)——龙卷接触伤另按原版 hostile ×2 规则结算(Projectile.cs:13781)。
518	/** v6 联机弹体打包元数据（Boss 锚定弹体族共用形状，Game.netProjBroadcast 消费） */
519	export interface NetProjMeta {
520	  kind: number;        // msg27 kind（5-8）
521	  tag: string;         // 锚参数打包（各类自定义，≤32 字符）
522	  aux: number;         // i16 辅助通道
523	  dmg: number;         // 快照 dmg 字段
524	  /** 可选：以 vx/vy 通道携带的角度方向（重建端 atan2 还原） */
525	  exVx?: number;
526	  exVy?: number;
527	}
528	
529	export class DukeSharknadoBolt extends Entity {
530	  w = 30; h = 30; // SetDefaults 385
531	  private vx0: number;
532	  private vy0: number;
533	  private big: boolean;
534	  private enraged: boolean;
535	  /** 小弹 vy 摆动相位(原版 ai[0]) */
536	  private phase = 0;
537	  private age = 0;
538	  life = 300; // SetDefaults timeLeft
539	  /** 鲨鱼生成所需的 Duke 引用(spawnPart 位置供体,实际落点由龙卷覆写) */
540	  private src: Enemy;
541	  dead = false;
542	
543	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, big: boolean, enraged: boolean, src: Enemy) {
544	    super();
545	    this.x = x - this.w / 2;
546	    this.y = y - this.h / 2;
547	    this.vx0 = vx; this.vy0 = vy;
548	    this.big = big; this.enraged = enraged;
549	    this.src = src;
550	  }
551	
552	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
553	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.kill(game); return; }
554	    this.age++;
555	    const p = game.player;
556	    if (this.big) {
557	      // :28405-28447 追踪:速 4(+12 狂暴)+localAI[0]/20,距玩家 <50px 即 Kill
558	      if (p && !p.dead) {
559	        const dx = p.cx - this.cx, dy = p.cy - this.cy;
560	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
561	        const spd = 4 + (this.enraged ? 12 : 0) + this.age / 20;
562	        this.vx0 = (dx / d) * spd;
563	        this.vy0 = (dy / d) * spd;
564	        if (d < 50) { this.kill(game); return; }
565	      }
566	    } else {
567	      // :28448-28475 vy 余弦摆动:vy += (cos(π/15·θₙ₊₁) − cos(π/15·θₙ))·4
568	      const f = (t: number) => (Math.cos((Math.PI / 15) * t) - 0.5) * 4;
569	      const d0 = f(this.phase);
570	      this.phase += 1;
571	      this.vy0 += f(this.phase) - d0;
572	    }
573	    this.x += this.vx0;
574	    this.y += this.vy0;
575	    // :28477-28481 入水上浮 16px 后 Kill
576	    const st = game.world.store;
577	    const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
578	    if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.liquid[st.idx(tx, ty)] > 0) { this.y -= 16; this.kill(game); return; }
579	    if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { this.kill(game); return; }
580	  }
581	
582	  /** Kill(:70714-70778):尘环 + 落龙卷(小 384 / 大 386 向下扫描地面)。
583	   *  v6 联机傀儡:运动/消亡本地自算(确定性),但不落龙卷——房主的龙卷经 kind6 同步,
584	   *  双端各自生成会重复 */
585	  private kill(game: GameHooks): void {
586	    this.dead = true;
587	    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#7FB8E8', 10, 2.4, { life: 24, damp: 0.94 });
588	    // :70716 弹 385 落地 = PlaySound(4,…,19) = NPC_Killed_19(case4=死音库,2026-08-19 终审补)
589	    game.playSfxWav?.('NPC_Killed_19', 1, this.cx, this.cy);
590	    if (this.netPuppet) return;
591	    if (this.big) {
592	      // :70752-70770 自弹体 tile 向下扫 100 格,落点 = 首个实心/液体格上沿 -24px
593	      const st = game.world.store;
594	      let tx = clamp(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), 10, st.w - 10);
595	      let ty = clamp(Math.floor(this.cy / TILE), 10, st.h - 110);
596	      let ground = ty + 15;
597	      for (let y = ty; y < ty + 100; y++) {
598	        if (st.inBounds(tx, y) && (st.isSolid(tx, y) || st.liquid[st.idx(tx, y)] !== 0)) { ground = y; break; }
599	      }
600	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoTornado(tx * TILE + 8, ground * TILE - 24, true, this.src));
601	    } else {
602	      // :70733-70737 原地落 384(中心反向偏移 direction*30, y-4)
603	      const dir = Math.sign(this.vx0) || 1;
604	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoTornado(this.cx - dir * 30, this.cy - 4, false, this.src));
605	    }
606	  }
607	
608	  /** v6 联机打包：kind=5，tag=big 标记（访客重建后本地跑确定性运动，见 kill 傀儡门禁）。
609	   *  终审 M1：速度经 exVx/exVy 通道携带（本类运动用私有 vx0/vy0，Entity.vx/vy 恒 0——
610	   *  不补则访客重建的小弹水平静止原地摆到寿终） */
611	  netProjMeta(): NetProjMeta {
612	    return { kind: 5, tag: this.big ? '1' : '0', aux: 0, dmg: 0, exVx: this.vx0, exVy: this.vy0 };
613	  }
614	
615	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
616	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
617	    if (!ctx) return;
618	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8FD8FF';
619	    ctx.beginPath();
620	    ctx.arc(this.cx, this.cy, 6, 0, TAU);
621	    ctx.fill();
622	  }
623	}
624	
625	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
626	// 鲨鱼龙卷 384 / 克苏龙卷 386(Projectile.cs:28287-28398,aiStyle 64)
627	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
628	// 原版是"父段 ai0 倒计时到 1 → 在正上方生成子段(ai0=10, ai1-1)直至 ai1=0"的投射物塔;
629	// 本实现以单实体模拟该塔:列顶随段生长上移,鲨鱼在"新段出现且 ai1 % 出鲨模 == 0"时
630	// 于列顶 NewNPC(小 384:模 4 → 3 只/塔;大 386:模 2 → 12 只/塔,ai2=幅宽 ai3=-1.5)。
631	// 接触伤害:小 40 / 大 80(经典 NewProjectile 值),命中按原版 hostile 弹 ×2 规则(:13781)。
632	export class DukeSharknadoTornado extends Entity {
633	  private big: boolean;
634	  private ai0: number;   // 段生长倒计时(父段 16,子段 10)
635	  private ai1: number;   // 剩余段数(小 15 / 大 24)
636	  private ai1Start: number;
637	  /** 列顶中心 y(鲨鱼生成点/绘制塔顶) */
638	  private topY: number;
639	  private segs = 0;      // 已生长段数
640	  private age = 0;
641	  private alpha = 255;   // 原版 alpha(实体内嵌 SolidCollision 逐渐显形)
642	  life: number;          // timeLeft 小 540 / 大 840
643	  private src: Enemy;
644	  dead = false;
645	
646	  constructor(x: number, y: number, big: boolean, src: Enemy) {
647	    super();
648	    this.big = big;
649	    this.ai0 = 16;                       // NewProjectile ai0
650	    this.ai1 = big ? 24 : 15;            // NewProjectile ai1
651	    this.ai1Start = this.ai1;
652	    this.topY = y;
653	    this.life = big ? 840 : 540;         // SetDefaults timeLeft
654	    this.src = src;
655	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
656	    this.resize();
657	  }
658	
659	  /** 段缩放(aiStyle 64 :28297-28316):小 (25-ai1)/25;大 (32-ai1)*1.5/32 */
660	  private scaleOf(ai1: number): number {
661	    return this.big ? (32 - ai1) * 1.5 / 32 : (25 - ai1) / 25;
662	  }
663	  private resize(): void {
664	    const s = this.scaleOf(this.ai1);
665	    this.w = Math.round(150 * s);        // SetDefaults 150×42 × scale
666	    this.h = Math.round(42 * s);
667	    this.x = this.cx - this.w / 2;
668	    this.y = this.topY - this.h / 2;
669	  }
670	
671	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
672	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
673	    this.age++;
674	    // alpha(:28336-28360):嵌实体块渐隐,否则向 60(大 100)显形
675	    const st = game.world.store;
676	    const inSolidTile = st.inBounds(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE)) && st.isSolid(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE));
677	    if (inSolidTile) this.alpha = Math.min(150, this.alpha + 30);
678	    else this.alpha = Math.max(this.big ? 100 : 60, this.alpha - 30);
679	
680	    if (this.ai0 > 0) this.ai0--;
681	    if (this.ai0 === 1 && this.ai1 > 0) {
682	      // :28363-28390 子段落点:列顶再上移 (42·sP + 42·sC)/2 − 2
683	      const sP = this.scaleOf(this.ai1), sC = this.scaleOf(this.ai1 - 1);
684	      this.topY -= (42 * sP) / 2 + (42 * sC) / 2 - 2;
685	      // 出鲨(:28372-28385):ai1 % 模 == 0 且 ai1≠0 → NewNPC 372/373 于列顶。
686	      // v6 傀儡不出鲨——房主的鲨鱼经 msg23 同步,双端各出会翻倍
687	      const mod = this.big ? 2 : 4;
688	      if (!this.netPuppet && this.ai1 % mod === 0 && this.ai1 !== 0) {
689	        const shark = spawnPart(game, this.src, this.big ? 373 : 372);
690	        if (shark) {
691	          shark.x = this.cx - shark.w / 2;
692	          shark.y = this.topY - shark.h / 2;
693	          shark.vx = this.vx; shark.vy = this.vy; // Main.npc.velocity = 弹体速度
694	          shark.bInit = false;                    // 由 sharkronAI 按 ai2/ai3 初始化
695	          shark.ai2 = this.big ? this.w : 0;      // 373 横游幅宽(:28382)
696	          shark.ai3 = this.big ? -1.5 : 0;        // 373 升速(:28383)
697	        }
698	      }
699	      this.ai1 -= 1;
700	      this.ai0 = 10;
701	      this.segs += 1;
702	      this.resize();
703	    }
704	    if (this.ai0 <= 0 && this.ai1 <= 0) {
705	      // :28392-28398 停止生长后横向摇摆(cos(π/30·−ai0) − 0.5)·幅宽
706	      const amp = (this.w / 5) * (this.big ? 2 : 1);
707	      const f = (t: number) => (Math.cos((Math.PI / 30) * t) - 0.5) * amp;
708	      this.x -= f(-this.ai0) * -1;
709	      this.ai0 -= 1;
710	      this.x += f(-this.ai0) * -1;
711	    }
712	
713	    // 接触伤害(hostile 弹 ×2 规则,Projectile.cs:13781:小 40→80 / 大 80→160)
714	    // 判定盒 = 整列(基座→列顶),宽取当前最宽段(= this.w,列顶段)
715	    const p = game.player;
716	    if (p && !p.dead) {
717	      const halfW = this.w / 2;
718	      const colTop = Math.min(this.topY, this.cy);
719	      const colH = Math.abs(this.cy - this.topY) + this.h;
720	      if (Math.abs(p.cx - this.cx) < halfW + p.w / 2 && Math.abs(p.cy - (colTop + colH / 2)) < colH / 2 + p.h / 2) {
721	        // 弹幕命中：attacker 仅做死亡归因，contactOnly 缺省 false → 荆棘不反伤
722	        // （反伤只在 NPC 接触分支 Player.cs:30910-30944）
723	        game.damagePlayer(this.big ? 160 : 80, this.cx, this.cy, this.src);
724	      }
725	    }
726	  }
727	
728	  /** v6 联机打包：kind=6，tag=`${big},${剩余段数ai1},${段倒计时ai0}`。
729	   *  近似收紧②：携带生长状态——中途入视野的龙卷按快照段盒+段数确定性恢复
730	   *  （topY = 快照y + 当前段半高,与房主同公式）,不再从零重长 */
731	  netProjMeta() {
732	    return { kind: 6, tag: `${this.big ? 1 : 0},${this.ai1},${this.ai0}`, aux: 0, dmg: 0 };
733	  }
734	
735	  /** v6 近似收紧②：访客重建时由快照段盒 + ai1/ai0 恢复生长态。
736	   *  topY 与 ai1 是确定性函数关系（resize: y = topY − h(ai1)/2），故由
737	   *  snapY 反解即可精确对齐房主当前态（含列顶位置,判定盒不再错位） */
738	  netStateSync(ai1: number, ai0: number, snapX: number, snapY: number): void {
739	    if (!Number.isFinite(ai1) || ai1 < 0 || ai1 > this.ai1Start) return; // 非法态保持新建
740	    this.ai1 = ai1;
741	    this.ai0 = Number.isFinite(ai0) ? ai0 : 10;
742	    this.segs = this.ai1Start - ai1;
743	    this.resize();
744	    this.x = snapX;                    // cx = snapX + w/2,resize 后 x = cx − w/2 = snapX
745	    this.topY = snapY + this.h / 2;    // 反解列顶（y = topY − h/2 的逆）
746	    this.y = snapY;
747	  }
748	
749	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
750	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
751	    if (!ctx) return;
752	    ctx.save();
753	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - this.alpha / 255;
754	    // 塔形:自基座向列顶逐段变宽(384 族贴图未解包,按段缩放画半透明白蓝柱)
755	    let segY = this.cy + this.h / 2;
756	    for (let i = this.segs; i >= 0; i--) {
757	      const s = this.scaleOf(Math.max(0, this.ai1Start - i));
758	      const sw = 150 * s, sh = 42 * s;
759	      segY -= sh;
760	      ctx.fillStyle = i % 2 ? '#9FD4F0' : '#7FB8E8';
761	      ctx.fillRect(this.cx - sw / 2, segY, sw, sh);
762	    }
763	    ctx.restore();
764	  }
765	}
766	
767	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
768	// 鲨鱼 NPC aiStyle 71(NPC 372 Sharkron / 373,NPC.cs:35196-35328)
769	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
770	// ai0=0:ai3 升速垂直蓄力 90t(dontTakeDamage;373 另带 ai2 幅宽余弦横游+渐显);
771	// 期满 ai0=1 → 16 速直扑玩家;ai0=1:渐显(−60/t)、撞实心块即死、60t 后恢复重力。
772	export function sharkronAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {
773	  if (!e.bInit) {
774	    e.bInit = true;
775	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0;
776	    e.spawnAlpha = 255; e.alphaFade = false; // 渐显由本 AI 按 :35228/:35267 驱动
777	  }
778	  // :35200-35205 玩家失效 → TargetClosest(faceTarget:false)+direction=1 重瞄,
779	  // AI 照常运转(原版对尸位坐标继续扑,不消散——曾 dead=true 即散 = 存续断裂,
780	  // 2026-08-19 台账 E 修;本仓单玩家无处重瞄 → 置 direction=1、维持现速滑行)
781	  if (!player || player.dead) {
782	    e.facing = 1;
783	    player = null;
784	  }
785	  const RISE_T = 90; // num1077
786	  if (e.ai0 === 0) {
787	    e.iframes = 2;              // dontTakeDamage(:35211)
788	    e.vx = 0;
789	    e.vy = e.ai3;               // velocity.Y = ai[3](:35212)
790	    if (e.vanillaId === 373) {
791	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 6); // :35228 渐显
792	      // :35222-35227 贴图倾斜 rotation = vy·spriteDirection·0.1(钳 ±0.2)
793	      e.spin = clamp(e.vy * -e.facing * 0.1, -0.2, 0.2);
794	    }
795	    e.ai1 += 1;
796	    if (e.ai1 >= RISE_T) {
797	      // :35242-35261 蓄力期满:16 速扑咬(撞块内则 ai1=0 延后起扑);
798	      // :35250 PlaySound(4,…,19)=**NPCKilled** style19(case4=死音库!曾误用
799	      // NPC_Hit_19 错库,2026-08-19 终审修)
800	      e.ai0 = 1;
801	      e.ai1 = inSolid(game, e) ? 0 : 1;
802	      game.playSfxFiles?.(['NPC_Killed_19'], 1, e.cx, e.cy);
803	      if (player) {
804	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
805	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
806	        e.vx = (dx / d) * 16;
807	        e.vy = (dy / d) * 16;
808	        e.facing = dx < 0 ? -1 : 1;
809	        e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) + (e.facing < 0 ? Math.PI : 0);
810	      }
811	    }
812	  } else {
813	    // ai0 === 1(:35264-35316)
814	    if (e.ai1 < 1 && !inSolid(game, e)) e.ai1 = 1;
815	    if (inSolid(game, e)) e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(150, e.spawnAlpha + 15); // :35280-35284
816	    if (e.ai1 >= 1) {
817	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 60); // :35288 渐显
818	      e.ai1 += 1;
819	      if (inSolid(game, e)) {
820	        // :35295-35303 撞墙即死:PlaySound(DeathSound)——372/373 SetDefaults
821	        // (:13666-13688)DeathSound=NPCDeath1 → NPC_Killed_1
822	        game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Killed_1'], 1, e.cx, e.cy);
823	        e.dead = true;
824	        return;
825	      }
826	    }
827	    if (e.ai1 >= 60) e.applyGravity(game); // :35307-35309 恢复重力(共享 UpdateNPC_UpdateGravity)
828	    e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) + (e.facing < 0 ? Math.PI : 0); // :35311-35315
829	  }
830	  // 373 蓄力段横游(:35213-35221):x += (cos(π/30·l1) − 0.5)·ai2·(−direction) 位移差
831	  if (e.ai0 === 0 && e.vanillaId === 373 && e.ai2 > 0) {
832	    const f = (t: number) => (Math.cos((Math.PI / 30) * t) - 0.5) * e.ai2;
833	    const prev = f(e.ai1 - 1), cur = f(e.ai1);
834	    e.x += (cur - prev) * -e.facing;
835	  }
836	  e.x += e.vx;
837	  e.y += e.vy;
838	}
839	
840	/** AI_070 爆裂泡泡(NPC 371,NPC.cs:35122-35195):
841	 *  朝玩家漂移(风偏+微上浮),玩家贴近(外扩 40px)或 150t 后爆炸(体积扩到 100×100,
842	 *  4t 后消散)。原版命中是 justHit→爆炸而非死亡;本工程近似为 hp 受损即转爆炸。
843	 *  简化:ai3 的随机 scale(:35134)无 Enemy 对应字段,仅记录不应用。 */
844	export function dukeBubbleAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
845	  void game;
846	  // :35180-35194 受击（justHit 单帧旗标）→ 无敌 + 爆炸体积；hp 损失本身不触发——
847	  // 真身是 StrikeNPC cs:82102-82107（371 伤害清零、ai0=1/ai1=4/dontTakeDamage），
848	  // 已由 Enemy.hurt() 同构处理，此处 justHit 为 AI 侧兜底
849	  if (e.ai0 !== 1 && e.justHit) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 4; }
850	  // ai0/ai1 复位先于任何读点(原在 player 门内——无玩家时 ai0 留 -1120 会让
851	  // :844 `ai0===0` 落空直入爆炸分支 1 tick 即死;2026-08-13 ai0 清查)
852	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }
853	  if (e.bInit && player && e.ai3 <= 0) { // :35124-35131 初速 = 朝玩家 ±100px 随机偏移 × 11~17.7 速
854	    e.ai3 = (80 + Math.random() * 41) / 100;
855	    const jx = e.cx - player.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;
856	    const jy = e.cy - player.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;
857	    const d = Math.hypot(jx, jy) || 1;
858	    const spd = (165 + Math.random() * 100) / 15;
859	    e.vx = -(jx / d) * spd;
860	    e.vy = -(jy / d) * spd;
861	    e.alphaFade = false;
862	    e.spawnAlpha = 50; // :35136-35140
863	  }
864	  if (player) {
865	    // :35132-35133 主漂移:v = (v*40 + dir*20)/41
866	    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
867	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
868	    e.vx = (e.vx * 40 + (dx / d) * 20) / 41;
869	    e.vy = (e.vy * 40 + (dy / d) * 20) / 41;
870	  }
871	  // :35141-35146 风扰+上浮(风取 0)
872	  e.vx = (e.vx * 50 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2) / 51;
873	  e.vy = (e.vy * 50 - 0.25 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4) / 51;
874	  if (e.vy > 0) e.vy -= 0.04;
875	  if (e.ai0 === 0 && player) { // :35147-35175 玩家贴近 → 爆炸
876	    const pad = 40 + e.w / 2;
877	    if (Math.abs(player.cx - e.cx) < pad + player.w / 2 && Math.abs(player.cy - e.cy) < pad + player.h / 2) {
878	      e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 4;
879	    }
880	  }
881	  if (e.ai0 === 0) {
882	    e.ai1 += 1;
883	    if (e.ai1 >= 150) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 4; } // :35167-35175 超时自爆
884	  } else {
885	    // 爆炸:体积扩到 100×100(:35187-35193)并 4t 后消散
886	    if (e.w !== 100) {
887	      e.x = e.cx - 50; e.y = e.cy - 50;
888	      e.w = 100; e.h = 100;
889	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#BFE8FF', 8, 2, { life: 20 });
890	    }
891	    e.iframes = 2; // dontTakeDamage
892	    e.ai1 -= 1;
893	    if (e.ai1 <= 0) { e.dead = true; return; }
894	  }
895	  e.x += e.vx;
896	  e.y += e.vy;
897	}
898	
899	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
900	// 月亮领主 aiStyle 77 核心(NPC 398,NPC.cs:36774-37198)
901	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
902	// ai0:-1 出生演出(60t,生成 2×397 手 + 1×396 头)→ 0 一阶段(双手+头存活期间无敌,随玩家悬停)
903	//   → 1 二阶段(三部件全部闭眼(ai0=-2)后解除无敌,自主追玩家+弹幕循环)
904	//   → 2 死亡演出(600t 后真死,全族消散)/ 3 玩家全灭离场(60t 后全族清除)。
905	//   -2:远距(>2400px)传送归位后的短演出。
906	
907	/** 二阶段弹幕表 = MoonLordAttacksArray2(:6966-6985,10 槽循环),驱动 NPC 400 真眼
908	 *  (aiStyle 81,:38243-38264,由部件 checkDead 召出,见 spawnTrueEye)。
909	 *  真眼数据/贴图缺失导致 spawn 失败时,攻击主链才折叠到核心上执行兜底。 */
910	const ML_CORE_P2: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]> = [
911	  [0, 90], [1, 90], [0, 90], [2, 135], [0, 90], [3, 200], [0, 90], [4, 375], [0, 90], [2, 135],
912	];
913	
914	/** 朝玩家发射一发弹幕的通用出口(伤害/弹 id 全部来自原版 NewProjectile 调用点) */
915	function mlShoot(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player, ox: number, oy: number,
916	  spd: number, dmg: number, projId: number) {
917	  const dx = player.cx - (e.cx + ox), dy = player.cy - (e.cy + oy);
918	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
919	  shoot(game, e.cx + ox, e.cy + oy, (dx / d) * spd, (dy / d) * spd, dmg, projId, 600);
920	}
921	
922	/** checkDead(:78845-78857):手 397 / 头 396 血尽 → ai0=-2 闭眼残肢的同时,于部件中心
923	 *  召 NPC 400 真眼,ai[3] = 部件的 ai[3](核心 id,游离眼 AI 据此锚定核心) */
924	function spawnTrueEye(game: GameHooks, part: Enemy): void {
925	  const eye = spawnPart(game, part, 400);
926	  if (!eye) return;
927	  eye.x = part.cx - eye.w / 2;
928	  eye.y = part.cy - eye.h / 2;
929	  eye.ai0 = 0; eye.ai1 = 0; eye.ai2 = 0; eye.ai3 = part.ai3;
930	}

932	/** 月噬弹 456（Projectile.cs:5008 SetDefaults + aiStyle 85 :30678-30750）：
933	 *  16×16 / hostile / 0 伤 / noTileCollide / timeLeft 600。ai[0]>0 出击段：每 tick 朝
934	 *  玩家 min(16, 距离) 直飞，贴身 <20px 时 AddBuff(145 MoonLeech, 840t/专家 960t)
935	 *  （:30722-30732，本仓经典模式恒 840t = 14s）；age≥330 或玩家失联 → ai[0] 翻负
936	 *  回程段：朝 额前(0,+216) 直飞，<20px Kill（吸血视觉闭环）。
937	 *  弹体自旋角 = (源心 - 玩家心 + (0,216)).ToRotation() + π/2（:30717）。 */
938	export class MoonLeechProj extends Entity {
939	  w = 16; h = 16;
940	  vx = 0; vy = 0;
941	  private srcId: number;
942	  private out = true;      // ai[0] 符号：true = 出击 / false = 回程
943	  private age = 0;         // localAI[0]
944	  private fed = false;     // localAI[1]（已吸血，不重复授 buff）
945	  private life = 600;      // timeLeft
946	  dead = false;
947	
948	  constructor(src: Enemy, player: Player) {
949	    super();
950	    this.srcId = src.id;
951	    this.x = src.cx - this.w / 2;
952	    this.y = src.cy + 216 - this.h / 2;   // 额前 (0,+216)
953	    const dx = player.cx - this.cx, dy = player.cy - this.cy;
954	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
955	    this.vx = (dx / d) * 6; this.vy = (dy / d) * 6;   // :37823 出速 6（AI 段接管为 16 上限）
956	  }
957	
958	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
959	    const src = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === this.srcId);
960	    const player = game.player;
961	    if (--this.life <= 0 || !src || src.dead) { this.dead = true; return; }
962	    if (!player || player.dead) { if (this.out) this.out = false; }   // :30706-30712 玩家失联即回程
963	    this.age++;
964	    if (this.age >= 330 && this.out) this.out = false;                // :30702-30705
965	    // 出击：朝玩家心直飞（:30722-30725）；回程：朝源额前 (0,+216)（:30734-30738）
966	    const tx = this.out ? player.cx : src.cx;
967	    const ty = this.out ? player.cy : src.cy + 216;
968	    const dx = tx - this.cx, dy = ty - this.cy;
969	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
970	    const spd = Math.min(16, d);
971	    if (d > 0.001) { this.vx = (dx / d) * spd; this.vy = (dy / d) * spd; }
972	    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
973	    // 贴身结算：出击段 <20px 授 MoonLeech 145（:30726-30732）；回程段 <20px Kill（:30740-30743）
974	    if (this.out && d < 20 && !this.fed) {
975	      this.fed = true;
976	      player.buffs.apply(BuffType.MoonLeech, 840 / 60);
977	    } else if (!this.out && d < 20) {
978	      this.dead = true;
979	    }
980	  }
981	
982	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
983	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
984	    if (!ctx) return;
985	    const img = projSprite(456);
986	    if (!img || !(img.width > 0) || img.width === 0) return;
987	    // 自旋（:30717）：(源心 - 玩家心 + (0,216)) 角 + π/2——回程矢量已同向，直接用速度角近似
988	    const ang = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI / 2;
989	    ctx.save();
990	    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
991	    ctx.rotate(ang);
992	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
993	    // 多帧行切片（Main.projFrames[456]=4，Main.cs:8490；aiStyle 85 不推帧 → 恒帧 0，
994	    // 帧格 22×24）。曾整条 22×96 压进 16×16 判定盒 = 胶片条（G11）
995	    const frames = projFrameCount(456);
996	    const fh = img.height / frames;
997	    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, img.width, fh, -img.width / 2, -fh / 2,
998	      img.width, fh);
999	    ctx.restore();
1000	  }
1001	}

1003	/** 月噬怪召出(:37828-37842 残肢 / :37975-37989 月噬攻击):头在 lt/inSlot=120/180/240
1004	 *  于玩家处召 NPC 401,ai[0]=头 whoAmI+1 / ai[1]=源弹 456 索引。
1005	 *  原版门禁 = 遍历全部 456 弹,其 ai[1] 指向的玩家 FindBuffIndex(145) != -1 才召
1006	 *  （每弹一只）。本仓单玩家:debuff 145 已由 MoonLeechProj 贴身授予 → 玩家身中
1007	 *  MoonLeech 即召,等价替换旧「弹已射出即命中」无条件近似
1008	 *  (ai[1] 源弹索引以 -1 代,moonLeechAI 以出生点代弹着点,不受影响)。 */
1009	function spawnMoonLeech(game: GameHooks, head: Enemy, player: Player): void {
1010	  if (!player.buffs.moonLeech) return;
1011	  const leech = spawnPart(game, head, 401);
1012	  if (!leech) return;
1013	  leech.x = player.cx - leech.w / 2;
1014	  leech.y = player.cy - leech.h / 2;
1015	  leech.ai0 = head.id + 1;
1016	  leech.ai1 = -1;
1017	  leech.ai2 = 0;
1018	  leech.vx = 0; leech.vy = 0;
1019	}

1021	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
1022	// 死亡之光 455 PhantasmalDeathray(Projectile.cs:30291-30549,aiStyle 84)
1023	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
1024	// 定轴扫射激光:锚定源 NPC 眼部(头 396 椭圆 (27,59)·睁眼度 / 真眼 400 椭圆 (30,30)),
1025	// 每 tick 转 ai0(=±2π/540),寿命 180t,scale = sin(age·π/180)·10·maxScale 脉冲张合;
1026	// 束长 LaserScan(3 采样,最大 2400)以 0.5/t Lerp 逼近;命中判定 = 玩家矩形 vs
1027	// 「源心 → 源心+dir·束长」线段,线宽 36·scale(Colliding :14673-14682);
1028	// 伤害 = NewProjectile 值 ×2(hostile 弹规则 :13781:头 75→150 / 真眼 50→100)。
1029	export class MLDeathray extends Entity {
1030	  w = 36; h = 36; // SetDefaults 455
1031	  private srcId: number;
1032	  /** v6 联机：锚定 NPC 的 netId（访客端锚=msg23 傀儡；0=本端真身走 srcId 查找） */
1033	  srcNetId = 0;
1034	  /** 'head' = 396 头眼 / 'core' = 400 真眼(本仓折叠到核心 398) */
1035	  private kind: 'head' | 'core';
1036	  /** 扫射角速度(原版 ai[0] = ±2π/540) */
1037	  private sweep: number;
1038	  /** 当前束角(原版 velocity.ToRotation,每 tick +sweep) */
1039	  private angle: number;
1040	  private age = 0;
1041	  /** 束长 Lerp 值(原版 localAI[1]) */
1042	  private len = 0;
1043	  private dmg: number;
1044	  dead = false;
1045	
1046	  constructor(src: Enemy | null, kind: 'head' | 'core', angle: number, sweep: number, dmg: number, x?: number, y?: number) {
1047	    super();
1048	    this.srcId = src?.id ?? 0;
1049	    this.kind = kind;
1050	    this.angle = angle;
1051	    this.sweep = sweep;
1052	    this.dmg = dmg;
1053	    // v6 傀儡重建：src=null 时由调用方传显式坐标（真身路径 src 必非空）
1054	    this.x = (x ?? src!.cx) - this.w / 2;
1055	    this.y = (y ?? src!.cy) - this.h / 2;
1056	  }
1057	
1058	  /** v6 联机打包：kind=7，tag=`${srcId},${0|1 数值位（0=head 1=core）}`，aux=扫速×10000，
1059	   *  束角经 vx/vy 通道携带（重建端 atan2 还原后本地扫射——确定性自包含） */
1060	  netProjMeta(): NetProjMeta {
1061	    return {
1062	      kind: 7,
1063	      tag: `${this.srcId},${this.kind === 'head' ? 0 : 1}`,
1064	      aux: Math.max(-32768, Math.min(32767, Math.round(this.sweep * 10000))),
1065	      dmg: this.dmg,
1066	      exVx: Math.cos(this.angle) * 1000,
1067	      exVy: Math.sin(this.angle) * 1000,
1068	    };
1069	  }
1070	
1071	  /** scale 上限:头 1.0 / 真眼 0.4(:30468-30478 num728) */
1072	  private get maxScale(): number { return this.kind === 'head' ? 1 : 0.4; }
1073	
1074	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
1075	    // v6 近似收紧①：束角回写——快照把房主实时角度放在 vx/vy 通道（exVx/exVy），
1076	    // 傀儡消费式同步（读后清零防下一 tick 重复回拉振荡）；快照间隙内本地按扫速
1077	    // 自推插值。后台页暂停恢复后由下一快照纠偏（此前只按首帧角自推会永久漂移）
1078	    if (this.netPuppet && (this.vx !== 0 || this.vy !== 0)) {
1079	      this.angle = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
1080	      this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
1081	    }
1082	    // v6 联机：傀儡端按 netId 找锚（msg23 傀儡）；本端真身按本地 id
1083	    const src = this.srcNetId
1084	      ? (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.netId === this.srcNetId)
1085	      : (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === this.srcId);
1086	    if (!src || src.dead) { this.dead = true; return; }        // 源消亡即 Kill(:30297)
1087	    if (!this.netPuppet && this.kind === 'head' && src.ai0 === -2) { this.dead = true; return; } // :30296 头闭眼残肢（ai0 不同步,傀儡跳过——msg28 兜底）
1088	    this.age++;
1089	    if (this.age >= 180) { this.dead = true; return; }         // :30472 寿命 180t
1090	    this.angle += this.sweep;                                  // :30521 rotation += ai[0]
1091	
1092	    // 锚点:束原点 = 源心 + 眼球椭圆偏移(:30293-30308)
1093	    const hx = headOf(src);
1094	    const open = this.kind === 'head' ? hx.l1 : 1;
1095	    const eyeA = this.kind === 'head' ? hx.l0 : this.angle;    // 真眼无独立瞳角,随束角
1096	    const ex = this.kind === 'head' ? Math.cos(eyeA) * 27 * open : Math.cos(eyeA) * 30;
1097	    const ey = this.kind === 'head' ? Math.sin(eyeA) * 59 * open : Math.sin(eyeA) * 30;
1098	    this.x = src.cx + ex - this.w / 2;
1099	    this.y = src.cy + ey - this.h / 2;
1100	
1101	    // 束长:LaserScan 3 采样(线宽 36·scale,最大 2400)均值,:30483-30509
1102	    const scale = Math.min(this.maxScale, Math.sin((this.age * Math.PI) / 180) * 10 * this.maxScale);
1103	    const wSq = 36 * scale;
1104	    const dirX = Math.cos(this.angle), dirY = Math.sin(this.angle);
1105	    let amt = 0.5;
1106	    let dist = this.laserScan(game, dirX, dirY, wSq);
1107	    const p = game.player;
1108	    if (this.kind === 'head' && p && !p.dead && !losClear(game, this.cx, this.cy, p.cx, p.cy)) {
1109	      // :30511-30519 头与玩家无视线 → 束长取 距玩家+150(防隔墙盲射)
1110	      dist = Math.min(2400, Math.hypot(p.cx - src.cx, p.cy - src.cy) + 150);
1111	      amt = 0.75;
1112	    }
1113	    this.len = lerp(this.len, dist, amt);
1114	
1115	    // 命中:玩家矩形 vs 束线段(线宽 36·scale),伤害走 damagePlayer(iframes 节流)。
1116	    // 弹幕命中：attacker 仅做死亡归因，contactOnly 缺省 false → 荆棘不反伤
1117	    // （反伤只在 NPC 接触分支 Player.cs:30910-30944）
1118	    if (p && !p.dead && segHitsRect(this.cx, this.cy, dirX * this.len, dirY * this.len, wSq, p)) {
1119	      game.damagePlayer(this.dmg, this.cx, this.cy, src);
1120	    }
1121	  }
1122	
1123	  /** Collision.LaserScan 近似:垂直束向 ±半宽 3 条采样线,8px 步进至首实心块 */
1124	  private laserScan(game: GameHooks, dirX: number, dirY: number, width: number): number {
1125	    const px = -dirY, py = dirX; // 垂直束向
1126	    let sum = 0;
1127	    for (let k = -1; k <= 1; k++) {
1128	      const off = (k * width) / 2;
1129	      const ox = this.cx + px * off, oy = this.cy + py * off;
1130	      const steps = Math.ceil(2400 / 8);
1131	      let d = 2400;
1132	      for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
1133	        const t = i * 8;
1134	        const tx = Math.floor((ox + dirX * t) / TILE), ty = Math.floor((oy + dirY * t) / TILE);
1135	        const st = game.world.store;
1136	        if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) { d = t - 8; break; }
1137	        if (st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { d = t - 8; break; }
1138	      }
1139	      sum += d;
1140	    }
1141	    return sum / 3;
1142	  }
1143	
1144	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
1145	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
1146	    if (!ctx) return;
1147	    const scale = Math.min(this.maxScale, Math.sin((this.age * Math.PI) / 180) * 10 * this.maxScale);
1148	    if (scale <= 0.01 || this.len <= 1) return;
1149	    const thick = Math.max(2, 36 * scale);
1150	    ctx.save();
1151	    ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(scale * 1.6, 0.25, 1);
1152	    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
1153	    ctx.rotate(this.angle);
1154	    ctx.fillStyle = '#B040D0';
1155	    ctx.fillRect(0, -thick / 2, this.len, thick);
1156	    ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(scale * 0.9, 0.15, 0.6);
1157	    ctx.fillStyle = '#F0C8FF';
1158	    ctx.fillRect(0, -thick / 4, this.len, thick / 2);
1159	    ctx.restore();
1160	  }
1161	}
1162	
1163	/** 线段 vs AABB(原版 Collision.CheckAABBvLineCollision 语义:线宽胶囊与矩形相交) */
1164	function segHitsRect(x0: number, y0: number, dx: number, dy: number, width: number,
1165	  t: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }): boolean {
1166	  // 最近点参数 t∈[0,1](钳到段内)
1167	  const cx = t.x + t.w / 2, cy = t.y + t.h / 2;
1168	  const len2 = dx * dx + dy * dy || 1;
1169	  let k = ((cx - x0) * dx + (cy - y0) * dy) / len2;
1170	  k = clamp(k, 0, 1);
1171	  const px = x0 + dx * k, py = y0 + dy * k;
1172	  // 最近点到矩形距离(逐轴钳位)
1173	  const nx = clamp(px, t.x, t.x + t.w), ny = clamp(py, t.y, t.y + t.h);
1174	  return Math.hypot(px - nx, py - ny) < width / 2;
1175	}
1176	
1177	/** 死亡之光发射出口(原版 :37902-37916 头 / :38625-38637 真眼):
1178	 *  起角 = 朝玩家角 ∓ 60°(num1278 = 玩家在左 +1),角速度 = num1278·2π/540。
1179	 *  返回源 NPC 的扫射编码角初值((起角+3π)·num1278,头部 ai2 逐 t 解码步进用)。 */
1180	function mlDeathray(game: GameHooks, src: Enemy, kind: 'head' | 'core', player: Player, dmg: number): number {
1181	  const dx = player.cx - src.cx, dy = player.cy - src.cy;
1182	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
1183	  const n = dx / d < 0 ? 1 : -1;                       // num1278
1184	  const ang = Math.atan2(dy, dx) - n * (TAU / 6);
1185	  addProj(game, new MLDeathray(src, kind, ang, n * (TAU / 540), dmg));
1186	  return (ang + Math.PI * 3) * n;
1187	}
1188	
1189	/** 核心悬停移动(:36854-36865 / :36912-36923 同构):目标 = 玩家 + (0,130),8 速/0.5 步进+半程混合 */
1190	function mlCoreHover(e: Enemy, player: Player) {
1191	  const dx = player.cx - e.cx;
1192	  const dy = player.cy + 130 - e.cy;
1193	  if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 20) {
1194	    const d = Math.hypot(dx - e.vx, dy - e.vy) || 1;
1195	    flyLerp(e, ((dx - e.vx) / d) * 8, ((dy - e.vy) / d) * 8, 0.5);
1196	  }
1197	}
1198	
1199	/** :36934-36952(死亡演出 ai1==60)/:37074-37099(玩家全灭离场 ai1==40)清弹段:
1200	 *  全部 456/462/455/452/454 弹 + NPC 400(原版前者 Kill()/后者 active=false——
1201	 *  本仓统一 dead=true 出桶) */
1202	function mlClearDeathrayProjectiles(game: GameHooks): void {
1203	  const ents = (game as unknown as {
1204	    entities?: { projectiles?: Array<{ dead?: boolean; projId?: number }> };
1205	  }).entities;
1206	  for (const p of ents?.projectiles ?? []) {
1207	    if (p.dead) continue;
1208	    const id = p.projId;
1209	    if (id === 456 || id === 462 || id === 455 || id === 452 || id === 454) p.dead = true;
1210	  }
1211	  for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
1212	    if (!n.dead && n.vanillaId === 400) n.dead = true;
1213	  }
1214	}
1215	
1216	export function moonLordCoreAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
1217	  // :36776-36778 1/200 环境吼(PlaySound 29 style Next(93,100) = Zombie_93..99,
1218	  // case29 93-99 文件级 ×0.4 自动档);出生(-1)与死亡演出(2)两态不吼
1219	  if (e.ai0 !== -1 && e.ai0 !== 2 && Math.floor(Math.random() * 200) === 0) {
1220	    game.playSfxWav?.(`Zombie_${93 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7)}`, 1, e.cx, e.cy);
1221	  }
1222	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = -1; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; } // :36780-36785
1223	  // 原版 checkDead(:78859-78867):血尽 → ai0=2 死亡演出,满血续命 600t 后真死
1224	  if (e.hp <= 0 && e.ai0 !== 2) { e.ai0 = 2; e.ai1 = 0; e.hp = e.maxHp; e.iframes = 2; }
1225	
1226	  if (e.ai0 === -2) { // 远距传送归位(:36786-36809)
1227	    e.iframes = 2;
1228	    e.ai1 += 1;
1229	    if (e.ai1 >= 60) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai0 = 0; }
1230	  } else if (e.ai0 === -1) { // 出生演出(:36810-36853)
1231	    e.iframes = 2;
1232	    e.ai1 += 1;
1233	    if (e.ai1 >= 60) {
1234	      e.ai1 = 0; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
1235	      // 生成双手 397(核心两侧 ±400)与头 396(上方 400);ai3 = 核心 id(锚定)
1236	      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
1237	        const h = spawnPart(game, e, 397);
1238	        if (h) {
1239	          h.x = e.cx + i * 800 - 400 - h.w / 2;
1240	          h.y = e.cy - 100 - h.h / 2;
1241	          h.ai0 = 0; h.ai1 = 0; h.ai2 = i; h.ai3 = e.id; h.bInit = true;
1242	        }
1243	      }
1244	      const hd = spawnPart(game, e, 396);
1245	      if (hd) {
1246	        hd.x = e.cx - hd.w / 2;
1247	        hd.y = e.cy - 400 - hd.h / 2;
1248	        hd.ai0 = 0; hd.ai1 = 0; hd.ai2 = 0; hd.ai3 = e.id; hd.bInit = true;
1249	      }
1250	      game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_92', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :36816 PlaySound(29,…,92)——case29 style92 ×0.5 自动档(曾借 roar 1.0)
1251	    }
1252	  } else if (e.ai0 === 0 || e.ai0 === 1) {
1253	    // 三部件(锚定本核心)
1254	    const parts = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).filter(
1255	      (n) => !n.dead && (n.vanillaId === 396 || n.vanillaId === 397) && n.ai3 === e.id);
1256	    if (e.ai0 === 0) {
1257	      e.iframes = 2; // dontTakeDamage(:36856)
1258	      // 部件缺失 → 核心即毁(:36866-36890 flag84)
1259	      if (parts.length < 3) { e.dead = true; return; }
1260	      // 三部件全部闭眼残肢(ai0=-2)→ 解除无敌进二阶段(:36891-36909 flag85)
1261	      if (parts.every((p) => p.ai0 === -2)) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
1262	    }
1263	    if (player) {
1264	      mlCoreHover(e, player);
1265	      // TargetClosest(faceTarget:false) 的 direction 语义(离场态 vx 目标用)
1266	      e.facing = player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : -1;
1267	    }
1268	    if (e.ai0 === 1 && player) {
1269	      // 原版二阶段核心只悬停(:36912-36923),攻击全部由真眼 400(aiStyle 81)执行;
1270	      // 真眼已由部件 checkDead 召出 → 不再走折叠弹幕(否则双倍)。真眼 spawn 失败
1271	      // (数据缺失)时保留折叠兜底
1272	      const eyes = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).some(
1273	        (n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 400 && n.ai3 === e.id);
1274	      if (!eyes) {
1275	        e.ai1 += 1;
1276	        mlCoreBarrage(e, game, player); // 二阶段弹幕(真眼主链折叠兜底)
1277	      }
1278	    }
1279	  } else if (e.ai0 === 2) { // 死亡演出(:36925-37064)
1280	    e.iframes = 2;
1281	    // :36928 命名参数陷阱——Lerp(value1: velocity, value2: (0,-0.5), amount: 0.98):
1282	    // 反编译显示参数序颠倒,实语义 = velocity = Lerp(velocity, (0,-0.5), 0.98),
1283	    // 即每 tick 98% 逼近 (0,-0.5)(曾误读 0.02 = 缓飘 50t 才到位)
1284	    e.vx = lerp(e.vx, 0, 0.98);
1285	    e.vy = lerp(e.vy, -0.5, 0.98);
1286	    e.ai1 += 1;
1287	    // :36934-36952 ai1==60 清全部 456/462/455/452/454 弹 + NPC 400(HitEffect 9999)
1288	    if (e.ai1 === 60) mlClearDeathrayProjectiles(game);
1289	    // :36954-37022 每 3t 一簇爆炎(60t<ai1<580);:37049-37051 ai1==1 NPCDeath61
1290	    if (e.ai1 === 1) game.playSfxFiles?.(['NPC_Killed_61'], 1, e.cx, e.cy);
1291	    if (e.ai1 % 3 === 0 && e.ai1 > 60 && e.ai1 < 580) {
1292	      game.spawnParticles(
1293	        e.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 400, e.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 400,
1294	        '#C04030', 6, 3, { life: 30 });
1295	    }
1296	    if (e.ai1 >= 600) { // :37053-37063 真死 → 全族由各部件 AI 检测 core.dead 自毁
1297	      e.dead = true;
1298	      return;
1299	    }
1300	  } else if (e.ai0 === 3) { // 玩家全灭离场(:37065-37135)
1301	    e.iframes = 2;
1302	    // :37068 同款命名参数陷阱:Lerp(velocity, (direction,-0.5), 0.98)——
1303	    // vx 目标 = direction(TargetClosest 持久朝向,由上方 0/1 态持续写入 facing)
1304	    e.vx = lerp(e.vx, e.facing, 0.98);
1305	    e.vy = lerp(e.vy, -0.5, 0.98);
1306	    e.ai1 += 1;
1307	    // :37074-37107 ai1==40 清全部 456/462/455/452/454 弹 + NPC 400(+gore 619-622)
1308	    if (e.ai1 === 40) mlClearDeathrayProjectiles(game);
1309	    if (e.ai1 >= 60) { // :37109-37127 清除全部部件与自身
1310	      for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
1311	        if (!n.dead && (n.vanillaId === 396 || n.vanillaId === 397 || n.vanillaId === 400) && n.ai3 === e.id) n.dead = true;
1312	      }
1313	      e.dead = true;
1314	      return;
1315	    }
1316	  }
1317	
1318	  // 玩家全灭 → 离场(:37136-37161)
1319	  if (!player && e.ai0 !== -1 && e.ai0 !== -2 && e.ai0 !== 2 && e.ai0 !== 3) { e.ai0 = 3; e.ai1 = 0; }
1320	  // 玩家远距 >2400px → 全族瞬移归位(:37162-37197)
1321	  if (player && e.ai0 >= 0 && e.ai0 < 2 && Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 2400) {
1322	    const dx = player.cx - e.cx;
1323	    const dy = player.cy - 150 - e.cy;
1324	    e.x += dx; e.y += dy;
1325	    for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
1326	      if (!n.dead && (n.vanillaId === 396 || n.vanillaId === 397 || n.vanillaId === 400) && n.ai3 === e.id) {
1327	        n.x += dx; n.y += dy;
1328	      }
1329	    }
1330	    e.ai0 = -2; e.ai1 = 0;
1331	  }
1332	  e.x += e.vx;
1333	  e.y += e.vy;
1334	}
1335	
1336	/** 核心兜底弹幕的螺旋眼角态(原版真眼 localAI[0]=眼角/ai[2]=角速度;核心折叠用) */
1337	interface MlBarrageX { ang: number; rate: number }
1338	const mlBarrageX = new WeakMap<Enemy, MlBarrageX>();
1339	const mlBarrageOf = (e: Enemy): MlBarrageX => {
1340	  let x = mlBarrageX.get(e);
1341	  if (!x) { x = { ang: 0, rate: 0 }; mlBarrageX.set(e, x); }
1342	  return x;
1343	};
1344	
1345	/** 核心二阶段弹幕(原版 NPC 400 真眼攻击链,aiStyle 81 :38243-38600;
1346	 *  攻击号语义按 MoonLordAttacksArray2 槽位(2026-08-19 台账 E 1↔3 归位):
1347	 *  1 = 三连幻影矢 462 35 伤(:38347-38384)——曾误接 452 环射;
1348	 *  3 = 幻影之眼 452 螺旋 35 伤(:38508-38572)——曾误接三连矢;
1349	 *  2 = 幻影能量球 454 40 伤(:38385-38506,近似);
1350	 *  4 = 死亡之光 455(:38580-38637) */
1351	function mlCoreBarrage(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player) {
1352	  const { atk, inSlot, dur } = attackSlot(e, ML_CORE_P2);
1353	  switch (atk) {
1354	    case 1: { // 三连幻影矢 462(:38347-38384):dur-35 音,dur-14/-7/末 三发 8 速 35 伤
1355	      const pvx = (player as unknown as { vx: number }).vx ?? 0;
1356	      const pvy = (player as unknown as { vy: number }).vy ?? 0;
1357	      const dx = player.cx + pvx * 20 - e.cx, dy = player.cy + pvy * 20 - e.cy;   // :38359 预判 20t
1358	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
1359	      if (inSlot === dur - 35) game.playSfxFiles?.(['NPC_Hit_6'], 1, e.cx, e.cy); // :38374-38377
1360	      if (inSlot === dur - 14 || inSlot === dur - 7 || inSlot === dur) {
1361	        shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy, dx / d * 8, dy / d * 8, 35, 462, 600);            // :38378-38382
1362	      }
1363	      break;
1364	    }
1365	    case 2: // 幻影能量球 454:先慢速散出,后段朝玩家加速(原版 30t 后转向寻的,:38447-38456/:38475)
1366	      if (inSlot === 0) {
1367	        for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
1368	          const ang = -Math.PI / 2 + (k - 1) * 0.6;
1369	          shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy, Math.cos(ang) * 4, Math.sin(ang) * 4, 40, 454, 600);
1370	        }
1371	      } else if (inSlot === 60) {
1372	        for (let k = -1; k <= 1; k++) mlShoot(e, game, player, 0, 0, 12, 40, 454);
1373	      }
1374	      break;
1375	    case 3: { // 幻影之眼 452 螺旋(:38508-38572):45..185 眼角自旋(±2π/40,每 40t ×0.95),
1376	      // 每 10t 沿眼角 8 速射一发 452(35 伤,出膛 = 中心 + 眼角方向 ×(30,30)长×0.4≈17px)
1377	      const bx = mlBarrageOf(e);
1378	      if (inSlot >= 45 && inSlot < 185) {
1379	        if (inSlot === 45) bx.rate = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 2) === 0 ? 1 : -1) * (TAU / 40);
1380	        if ((inSlot - 45) % 40 === 0) bx.rate *= 0.95;   // :38540-38543
1381	        bx.ang += bx.rate;
1382	        if ((inSlot - 45) % 10 === 0) {                  // :38554-38559
1383	          const ux = Math.cos(bx.ang), uy = Math.sin(bx.ang);
1384	          shoot(game, e.cx + ux * 17, e.cy + uy * 17, ux * 8, uy * 8, 35, 452, 600);
1385	        }
1386	      }
1387	      break;
1388	    }
1389	    case 4: // 死亡之光 455(:38625-38647):inSlot==180 一发定轴扫射激光(50 伤→命中×2=100)
1390	      // 原版此槽 375t:0-179 蓄能尘,180 发束,束体自转 180t 至槽末;真眼瞳角动画随束同步
1391	      // (localAI[0],:38642-38652)——真眼不在本仓范围,锚点用核心中心 (30,30) 椭圆近似
1392	      if (inSlot === 180) { e.ai2 = mlDeathray(game, e, 'core', player, 50 * 2); }
1393	      break;
1394	    default: break; // 0 = 闭眼冷却 90t
1395	  }
1396	}
1397	
1398	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
1399	// 月亮领主 aiStyle 78 手(NPC 397,NPC.cs:37199-37711)
1400	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
1401	// 攻击时间表 MoonLordAttacksArray[0, ai2](:6854-6883):
1402	//   ai2=0(左手,side=-1):[闭50, 眼70, 球330, 闭60, 矢90]
1403	//   ai2=1(右手,side=+1):[眼70, 闭50, 矢90, 闭60, 球330]
1404	// 攻击:0 闭眼悬停(无敌)/ 1 眼球环射 452(30 伤)/ 2 能量球扫射 454(40 伤,末段全数转向玩家)
1405	//   / 3 三连幻影矢 462(30 伤,预判 20t)。
1406	// 无敌语义:dontTakeDamage = frame>=21(:37215)——只有攻击中(睁眼)可受击。
1407	
1408	const ML_HAND_ATTACKS: Readonly<Record<number, ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]>>> = {
1409	  0: [[0, 50], [1, 70], [2, 330], [0, 60], [3, 90]],
1410	  1: [[1, 70], [0, 50], [3, 90], [0, 60], [2, 330]],
1411	};
1412	
1413	export function moonLordHandAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
1414	  // 锚定核心;核心缺失/死亡 → 自毁(:37202-37207)
1415	  const core = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === e.ai3 && n.vanillaId === 398);
1416	  if (!core || core.dead) { e.dead = true; return; }
1417	  // 原版 checkDead(:78845-78857):血尽 → ai0=-2 闭眼残肢(满血续命+无敌)+召真眼 400
1418	  if (e.hp <= 0 && e.ai0 !== -2) {
1419	    e.ai0 = -2; e.ai1 = 0; e.hp = e.maxHp; e.iframes = 2;
1420	    spawnTrueEye(game, e);
1421	  }
1422	
1423	  const x = handOf(e);
1424	  const side = e.ai2 === 0 ? -1 : 1;  // num1231 :37208-37210
1425	  e.facing = side;                    // spriteDirection
1426	
1427	  // 攻击表推进(:37221-37251;ai0=-2 残肢态不参与调度)
1428	  let inSlot = 0, dur = 0;
1429	  if (e.ai0 !== -2) {
1430	    e.ai1 += 1;
1431	    const s = attackSlot(e, ML_HAND_ATTACKS[e.ai2 === 0 ? 0 : 1]);
1432	    e.ai0 = s.atk; // :37245 原版 ai[0] 即攻击号(亦为手部状态)
1433	    inSlot = s.inSlot; dur = s.dur;
1434	  }
1435	  let frameT = 0; // num1234(帧目标=frameT*7)
1436	
1437	  if (e.ai0 === -2) { // 闭眼残肢(:37252-37280)
1438	    frameT = 0;
1439	    e.def.damage = 80; // :37254
1440	    if (e.ai1 >= 32) e.ai1 = 0;
1441	    handHover(e, core, side);
1442	  } else if (e.ai0 === 0) { // 闭眼悬停(:37281-37303)
1443	    frameT = 3;
1444	    x.l1 = Math.max(0, x.l1 - 0.05);
1445	    handHover(e, core, side);
1446	  } else if (e.ai0 === 1 && player) { // 眼球环射(:37304-37346)
1447	    frameT = 0;
1448	    const cyc = 28; // num1240*num1241 = 7*4
1449	    if (inSlot >= cyc * 2) {
1450	      x.l1 = Math.max(0, x.l1 - 0.07);
1451	    } else if (inSlot >= cyc) {
1452	      x.l1 = Math.min(0.75, x.l1 + 0.05);
1453	      const ang = (TAU * (inSlot % cyc)) / cyc - Math.PI / 2; // :37324
1454	      x.l0 = Math.atan2(Math.sin(ang) * 66, Math.cos(ang) * 30);
1455	      if (inSlot % 4 === 0) handFireEye(e, game, x, side); // :37326-37334 452(30 伤)
1456	    } else {
1457	      x.l1 = Math.min(0.75, x.l1 + 0.02);
1458	      const ang = (TAU * (inSlot % cyc)) / cyc - Math.PI / 2;
1459	      x.l0 = Math.atan2(Math.sin(ang) * 66, Math.cos(ang) * 30);
1460	    }
1461	  } else if (e.ai0 === 2) { // 能量球扫射(:37347-37447)
1462	    const ax = core.cx + 220 * side + side * 100;   // vector168
1463	    const ay = core.cy - 60 - 50;
1464	    const swx = 400 * side, swy = -60;              // vector169
1465	    if (inSlot < 30) {
1466	      handApproach(e, ax, ay, 8, 0.2);
1467	    } else if (inSlot < 210) {
1468	      frameT = 1;
1469	      if ((inSlot - 30) % 30 === 0 && player) { // :37372-37380 每 30t 一发 454(40 伤)
1470	        const k = Math.floor((inSlot - 30) / 30);
1471	        let vx = 5 * side + (k - 3.5) * side * 3;
1472	        let vy = -8 + (k - 4.5);
1473	        vx *= 1.2; vy *= 1.2;
1474	        shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy, vx, vy, 40, 454, 600);
1475	      }
1476	      const t = smoothAmt((inSlot - 30) / 180);
1477	      handSeek(e, ax + swx * t, ay + swy * t, 20, 0.5); // :37381-37387
1478	    } else if (inSlot < 282) {
1479	      frameT = 0;
1480	      e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9;
1481	    } else if (inSlot < 287) {
1482	      frameT = 1;
1483	      e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9;
1484	    } else if (inSlot < 292) {
1485	      frameT = 2;
1486	      e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9;
1487	    } else if (inSlot < 300) {
1488	      frameT = 3;
1489	      // :37407-37427 原版在此把本手全部 454 弹转向玩家(12 速);本工程投射物无寻的,
1490	      // 近似为朝玩家补射 6 发 454(40 伤)
1491	      if (inSlot === 292 && player) {
1492	        for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
1493	          const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
1494	          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
1495	          const ang = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + (k - 2.5) * 0.18;
1496	          shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy, Math.cos(ang) * 12, Math.sin(ang) * 12, 40, 454, 600);
1497	        }
1498	      }
1499	      const t = 1 - (inSlot - 270) / 30;
1500	      handSeek(e, ax + swx * smoothAmt(t), ay + swy * smoothAmt(t), 14, 0.1); // :37428-37434
1501	    } else {
1502	      frameT = 3;
1503	      handApproach(e, ax, ay, 8, 0.2); // :37436-37446 归位
1504	    }
1505	  } else if (e.ai0 === 3 && player) { // 三连幻影矢(:37448-37472)
1506	    frameT = 0;
1507	    const pvx = (player as unknown as { vx: number }).vx ?? 0;
1508	    const pvy = (player as unknown as { vy: number }).vy ?? 0;
1509	    const dx = player.cx + pvx * 20 - e.cx, dy = player.cy + pvy * 20 - e.cy; // :37455 预判 20t
1510	    x.l0 = angleLerp(x.l0, Math.atan2(dy, dx), 0.5);
1511	    x.l1 = Math.min(1, x.l1 + 0.05);
1512	    if (inSlot === dur - 35) game.playSfxWav?.('NPC_Hit_6', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :37464 PlaySound(4,…,6)=NPCHit style6(曾借 roar 0.5)
1513	    if (inSlot === dur - 14 || inSlot === dur - 7 || inSlot === dur) {
1514	      // :37466-37471 从眼球位置射 462(30 伤,8 速)
1515	      const exx = Math.cos(x.l0) * 30 * x.l1, eyy = Math.sin(x.l0) * 66 * x.l1;
1516	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
1517	      shoot(game, e.cx + exx, e.cy + eyy, (dx / d) * 8, (dy / d) * 8, 30, 462, 600);
1518	    }
1519	  }
1520	
1521	  // 睁闭眼帧推进(:37493-37509):每 tick 向目标帧走 1,dontTakeDamage = frame>=21
1522	  const frameTarget = frameT * 7;
1523	  if (frameTarget > x.frame) x.frame += 1;
1524	  else if (frameTarget < x.frame) x.frame -= 1;
1525	  x.frame = clamp(x.frame, 0, 21);
1526	
1527	  // 位置钳制(:37473-37492):下一位置锁在核心侧上方盒内
1528	  let minX = core.cx + 220 * side + 110 * side, minY = core.cy - 60 - 150;
1529	  let maxX = minX + 370 * side, maxY = minY + 150;
1530	  if (minX > maxX) { const t = minX; minX = maxX; maxX = t; }
1531	  if (minY > maxY) { const t = minY; minY = maxY; maxY = t; }
1532	  const nx = clamp(e.cx + e.vx, minX, maxX);
1533	  const ny = clamp(e.cy + e.vy, minY, maxY);
1534	  e.x = nx - e.w / 2;
1535	  e.y = ny - e.h / 2;
1536	
1537	  // 无敌:闭眼(frame>=21)或残肢态;可受击时不写 iframes,保留 hurt() 的 8t 受击免疫
1538	  if (x.frame >= 21 || e.ai0 === -2) e.iframes = 2;
1539	  e.spin = x.l0; // 手部眼球角度(渲染朝向)
1540	}
1541	
1542	/** 手部悬停(:37266-37279 / :37289-37302):核心侧上方 (350*side, -100),6 速/0.3 步进+半程混合 */
1543	function handHover(e: Enemy, core: Enemy, side: number) {
1544	  const dx = core.cx + 350 * side - e.cx;
1545	  const dy = core.cy - 100 - e.cy;
1546	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
1547	  if (d > 20) flyLerp(e, (dx / d) * 6, (dy / d) * 6, 0.3);
1548	}
1549	/** SmoothStep 趋近(:37358-37367 / :37438-37445):velocity = SmoothStep(v, normalize(delta)*min(spd,|delta|), k) */
1550	function handApproach(e: Enemy, tx: number, ty: number, spd: number, k: number) {
1551	  const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
1552	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
1553	  if (d < 0.01) return;
1554	  const m = Math.min(spd, d);
1555	  e.vx = vSmoothStep(e.vx, (dx / d) * m, k);
1556	  e.vy = vSmoothStep(e.vy, (dy / d) * m, k);
1557	}
1558	/** 扫射段追随(:37381-37387 / :37428-37434):Lerp(v, normalize(delta)*min(spd,|delta|), k) */
1559	function handSeek(e: Enemy, tx: number, ty: number, spd: number, k: number) {
1560	  const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
1561	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
1562	  if (d < 0.01) return;
1563	  e.vx = lerp(e.vx, (dx / d) * Math.min(spd, d), k);

168	    if (player) e.facing = e.seekDirX(player);
169	    const dx = pcx + (player ? player.vx : 0) * 20 - e.cx;
170	    const dy = pcy + (player ? player.vy : 0) * 20 - e.cy;
171	    x[0] = angleLerp(x[0], Math.atan2(dy, dx), 0.5);
172	    x[1] = Math.min(0.7, x[1] + 0.05);
173	    x[2] = lerp(x[2], 1, 0.2);
174	    let tx = pcx - e.cx, ty = pcy - 200 - e.cy;
175	    const d = Math.hypot(tx, ty) || 1;
176	    tx = (tx / d) * 24; ty = (ty / d) * 24;
177	    e.vx = (e.vx * 29 + tx) / 30;
178	    e.vy = (e.vy * 29 + ty) / 30;
179	    for (const n of enemies(game)) {                      // :38327-38345
180	      if (n !== e && !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 400
181	        && Math.hypot(n.cx - e.cx, n.cy - e.cy) < 150) {
182	        if (e.x < n.x) e.vx -= 0.25; else e.vx += 0.25;
183	        if (e.y < n.y) e.vy -= 0.25; else e.vy += 0.25;
184	      }
185	    }
186	  } else if (e.ai0 === 1) {
187	    // ---- 幻影矢 462(:38347-38395):减速瞄准,dur-14/-7/dur 三连(35 伤,速 8) ----
188	    if (t === 0 && player) e.facing = e.seekDirX(player);
189	    e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
190	    if (Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) < 1) { e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
191	    const dx = pcx + (player ? player.vx : 0) * 20 - e.cx;
192	    const dy = pcy + (player ? player.vy : 0) * 20 - e.cy;
193	    x[0] = angleLerp(x[0], Math.atan2(dy, dx), 0.5);
194	    x[1] = Math.min(1, x[1] + 0.05);
195	    x[2] = lerp(x[2], t < 20 ? 1.1 : 0.4, 0.2);
196	    if (t === dur - 35) game.playSfxWav?.('NPC_Hit_6', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :38389 PlaySound(4,…,6)=NPCHit style6(曾借 roar 0.3)
197	    if (t === dur - 14 || t === dur - 7 || t === dur) {          // :38392-38398
198	      const ox = Math.cos(x[0]) * 30 * x[1], oy = Math.sin(x[0]) * 30 * x[1];
199	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
200	      shootL(game, e.cx + ox, e.cy + oy, (dx / d) * 8, (dy / d) * 8, 35, 462);
201	    }
202	  } else if (e.ai0 === 2) {
203	    // ---- 幻影球 454 六角螺旋(:38397-38491) ----
204	    if (t < 15) {                                            // :38398-38406 收瞳减速
205	      x[1] = Math.max(0, x[1] - 0.07);
206	      x[2] = lerp(x[2], 0.4, 0.2);
207	      e.vx *= 0.8; e.vy *= 0.8;
208	      if (Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) < 1) { e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
209	    } else if (t < 75) {
210	      // :38408-38460 六顶点两两插值轨道(0→2→5→3→1→4→0),每 10t 沿轨道外发 454(40 伤,速 4)
211	      const k = Math.floor((t - 15) / 10);
212	      const PAIRS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]> = [[0, 2], [2, 5], [5, 3], [3, 1], [1, 4], [4, 0]];
213	      const [p0, p1] = PAIRS[k] ?? [0, 2];
214	      const hex = (i: number): [number, number] => {
215	        const a = -Math.PI / 2 + (i * TAU) / 6;             // Vector2.UnitY*-30 旋转 i·π/3
216	        return [Math.cos(a) * 30, Math.sin(a) * 30];
217	      };
218	      const f = (t - 15) / 10 - k;
219	      const [ax, ay] = hex(p0), [bx, by] = hex(p1);
220	      const px = lerp(ax, bx, f), py = lerp(ay, by, f);
221	      x[0] = Math.atan2(py, px);
222	      x[1] = lerp(x[1], Math.hypot(px, py) / 30, 0.5);
223	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx + px, e.cy + py, '#C040D0', 2, 0.8, { life: 20 });  // :38455-38459
224	      if ((t - 15) % 10 === 0) {
225	        const d = Math.hypot(px, py);
226	        const ux = d > 0.001 ? px / d : 0, uy = d > 0.001 ? py / d : -1;
227	        shootL(game, e.cx + px, e.cy + py, ux * 4, uy * 4, 40, 454, 600);
228	      }
229	    } else if (t < 105) {
230	      // :38462-38485 瞄准升空(75t 起跳 vy=-7);原版同步给已发 454 叠加速度,Arrow 无弹 ai 槽,略
231	      x[0] = angleLerp(x[0], e.ai2 - Math.PI / 2, 0.2);
232	      x[2] = lerp(x[2], 0.75, 0.2);
233	      if (t === 75) { e.vx = 0; e.vy = -7; }
234	      e.vy *= 0.96;
235	      if (player) e.ai2 = Math.atan2(pcy - e.cy, pcx - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
236	      e.spin = angleTowards(e.spin, e.ai2, Math.PI / 30);
237	    } else if (t < 120) {
238	      // :38487-38505 发射:沿瞄准向 24 速冲出;已发 454 改归巢(ai[0]=-1),略
239	      game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_102', 1, e.cx, e.cy);        // :38487 PlaySound(29,…,102)——case29 style102 ×0.4 自动档(曾借 roar 0.4 恰同值)
240	      e.vx = Math.cos(e.ai2 - Math.PI / 2) * 24;
241	      e.vy = Math.sin(e.ai2 - Math.PI / 2) * 24;
242	    } else {
243	      e.vx *= 0.92; e.vy *= 0.92;                            // :38507-38510
244	      e.spin = angleLerp(e.spin, 0, 0.2);
245	    }
246	  } else if (e.ai0 === 3) {
247	    // ---- 幻影眼 452 洒射(:38512-38583) ----
248	    if (t < 15) {
249	      x[1] = Math.max(0, x[1] - 0.07);
250	      x[2] = lerp(x[2], 0.4, 0.2);
251	      e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9;
252	      if (Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) < 1) { e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
253	    } else if (t < 45) {
254	      // :38530-38538 瞳孔摆正
255	      x[0] = 0;
256	      x[1] = Math.sin((t - 15) * TAU / 15) * 0.5;
257	      if (x[1] < 0) x[0] = Math.PI;
258	    } else if (t < 185) {
259	      // :38540-38568 螺旋外扩洒射(8→20 速,每 10t 一发 452,35 伤,速 8)
260	      if (t === 45) e.ai2 = (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1) * TAU / 40;
261	      if ((t - 45) % 40 === 0) e.ai2 *= 0.95;
262	      x[0] += e.ai2;
263	      x[1] = Math.min(1, x[1] + 0.05);
264	      const ox = Math.cos(x[0]) * 30 * x[1], oy = Math.sin(x[0]) * 30 * x[1];
265	      const spd = lerp(8, 20, (t - 45) / 140);
266	      const d = Math.hypot(ox, oy) || 1;
267	      e.vx = (ox / d) * spd; e.vy = (oy / d) * spd;
268	      e.spin = angleLerp(e.spin, Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) + Math.PI / 2, 0.2);
269	      if ((t - 45) % 10 === 0) {
270	        // :38557-38563 弹 ai[3]=随机弧线扰动,Arrow 无 ai 槽,略
271	        shootL(game, e.cx + (ox / d) * 12, e.cy + (oy / d) * 12,
272	          (ox / d) * 8, (oy / d) * 8, 35, 452, 600);
273	      }
274	    } else {
275	      // :38570-38583 收势
276	      e.vx *= 0.88; e.vy *= 0.88;
277	      e.spin = angleLerp(e.spin, 0, 0.2);
278	      x[1] = Math.max(0, x[1] - 0.07);
279	      x[2] = lerp(x[2], 1, 0.2);
280	    }
281	  } else if (e.ai0 === 4) {
282	    // ---- 死亡之光 455(:38585-38665):180t 吸能 → MLDeathray('core' 档,50 伤×2) ----
283	    if (t === 0 && player) e.facing = e.seekDirX(player);
284	    if (t < 180) {
285	      x[2] = lerp(x[2], 1, 0.2);
286	      x[1] = Math.max(0, x[1] - 0.05);
287	      e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
288	      if (Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) < 1) { e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
289	      if (t >= 60) {                                          // :38616-38632 吸能粒子
290	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 30, e.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 30,
291	          '#B040D0', t >= 120 ? 2 : 1, 1.2, { life: 24 });
292	      }
293	    } else if (t < dur - 15) {
294	      if (t === 180 && player) {
295	        // :38634-38650 起角 = 朝玩家 ∓ 60°,角速度 = ±2π/540(MLDeathray 'core' 档 1:1)
296	        const dx = pcx - e.cx, dy = pcy - e.cy;
297	        const dd = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
298	        const n = dx / dd < 0 ? 1 : -1;                       // num1318
299	        const ang = Math.atan2(dy, dx) - n * TAU / 6;
300	        (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (en: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(
301	          new MLDeathray(e, 'core', ang, n * TAU / 540, 50 * 2), 'projectiles');
302	        e.ai2 = (ang + Math.PI * 3) * n;                      // :38649 扫射编码角
303	      }
304	      x[1] = Math.min(1, x[1] + 0.05);
305	      // :38656-38663 编码角逐 t 解码步进(瞳角跟踪扫射束)
306	      const s = e.ai2 >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
307	      const a = Math.abs(e.ai2) - Math.PI * 3 + (s * TAU) / 540;
308	      x[0] = a;
309	      e.ai2 = (a + Math.PI * 3) * s;
310	    } else {
311	      x[1] = Math.max(0, x[1] - 0.07);                        // :38664-38666
312	    }
313	  }
314	  fly(e);
315	}

317	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
318	// AI_082 月总水蛭块 401(NPC.cs:38668-38792)
319	// ai[0]=头 396 id+1 / ai[1]=源弹索引(本仓 Arrow 无索引,以出生点代源弹 456 弹着点);
320	// ai[2] 90t 插值 头额(0,+216);到位 1000 点治疗按 头→核心→手0→手1 亏血序回灌后消散。
321	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
322	export function moonLeechAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, _player: Player | null) {
323	  const x = lunarLocalAI(e);
324	  if (!e.bInit) {
325	    e.bInit = true;
326	    e.ai2 = 0;
327	    x[0] = e.cx; x[1] = e.cy;    // 源弹 456 弹着点(=玩家处)近似:出生点即弹着点(:37832)
328	  }
329	  // ---- 头锚定(:38676-38684) ----
330	  const head = enemies(game).find((n) => n.id === Math.floor(e.ai0) - 1 && n.vanillaId === 396);
331	  if (!head || head.dead) { e.dead = true; return; }
332	  e.ai2 += 1;
333	  const LEECH_T = 90;                                       // num1321
334	  if (e.ai2 >= LEECH_T) {
335	    // ---- 治疗回灌(:38689-38772):池 1000,按 头→核心→手0→手1 亏血序 ----
336	    const core = enemies(game).find((n) => n.id === head.ai3 && n.vanillaId === 398);
337	    const hands = enemies(game).filter((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 397 && n.ai3 === head.ai3);
338	    const hand0 = hands.find((n) => n.ai2 === 0);
339	    const hand1 = hands.find((n) => n.ai2 === 1);
340	    let pool = 1000;                                        // num1329
341	    for (const tgt of [head, core, hand0, hand1]) {
342	      if (!tgt || tgt.dead || pool <= 0) continue;
343	      const deficit = tgt.maxHp - tgt.hp;
344	      if (deficit <= 0) continue;
345	      const amt = Math.min(pool, deficit);                  // num1335 = pool+min(deficit-pool,0)
346	      tgt.hp += amt;
347	      pool -= amt;
348	      game.spawnParticles(tgt.cx, tgt.cy, '#5AE86A',
349	        Math.min(8, 2 + Math.floor(amt / 100)), 1.6, { life: 30 });   // HealEffect 近似
350	    }
351	    e.dead = true;                                          // :38770-38772 静默消散
352	    return;
353	  }
354	  // ---- 插值飞行(:38775-38791):出生点 → 头额 ----
355	  e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
356	  const k = e.ai2 / LEECH_T;
357	  e.x = lerp(x[0], head.cx, k) - e.w / 2;
358	  e.y = lerp(x[1], head.cy + 216, k) - e.h / 2;
359	  game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy - e.h / 2, '#B040D0', 3, 0.7, { life: 20 });   // 紫尘双环
360	}
361	
362	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
363	// AI_083a 神秘石碑 437(NPC.cs:38795-38920 + CultistRitual.cs CheckFloor)
364	// 落地校验(CheckFloor:±6/±10 格四点 3 格净空)→ 召 4 教徒;信徒受击→弓手激活;
365	// 四教徒全灭 → 侧面召 439 + 300t 仪式倒计时(120t 起每 10t 仪式粒子)消散。
366	// 槽位:弓手 id+1 存 ai[0..1](原版同),信徒 id+1 存 localAI[1..2];ai[0]=-1 标记
367	// 邪教徒已召(此后 ai[3] 为倒计时)。
368	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
369	/** CultistRitual.CheckFloor(CultistRitual.cs:88-124 1:1):i∈{-5,-3,3,5} → x=tileX+i*2,
370	 *  y 自 tileY-5 向下扫至 +11,取首个"实心/平台 且上方 3 格净空"的点;不足 4 点 → null */
371	export function ritualCheckFloor(game: GameHooks, cx: number, cy: number): Array<{ x: number; y: number }> | null {
372	  const pts: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
373	  const tx = Math.floor(cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(cy / TILE);
374	  for (const i of [-5, -3, 3, 5]) {
375	    const x = tx + i * 2;
376	    for (let j = -5; j < 12; j++) {
377	      const y = ty + j;
378	      if (y < 1 || y >= game.world.store.h - 1) break;
379	      const floor = solidTileAt(game, x, y) || game.world.store.isPlatform(x, y);
380	      if (!floor) continue;
381	      // 净空:本柱 3 格全空,或(本柱空 且 左右柱上两行空)——原版双条件或语义
382	      const clearMid = !solidRect(game, x - 1, x + 1, y - 3, y - 1);
383	      const clearSide = !solidRect(game, x, x, y - 3, y - 1)
384	        && !solidRect(game, x + 1, x + 1, y - 3, y - 2)
385	        && !solidRect(game, x - 1, x - 1, y - 3, y - 2);
386	      if (clearMid || clearSide) { pts.push({ x, y }); break; }
387	    }
388	  }
389	  return pts.length === 4 ? pts : null;
390	}
391	
392	export function cultistTabletAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, _player: Player | null) {
393	  const x = lunarLocalAI(e);
394	  e.iframes = 2;                       // SetDefaults dontTakeDamage=true(:14115 段)
395	  e.lightRGB = [0.8, 0.75, 0.55];      // :38957 Lighting.AddLight
396	  if (!e.bInit) {
397	    // ---- 落地校验 + 召四教徒(:38798-38824) ----
398	    e.bInit = true;
399	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
400	    const pts = ritualCheckFloor(game, e.cx, e.cy);
401	    if (!pts) { e.dead = true; return; }               // 站不住 → 静默消散
402	    let arcN = 0, devN = 0;
403	    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
404	      const isDevotee = k === 1 || k === 2;            // 内侧两点信徒,外侧两点弓手
405	      const child = spawnPart(game, e, isDevotee ? 438 : 379);
406	      if (!child) continue;
407	      child.x = pts[k].x * TILE + 8 - child.w / 2;
408	      child.y = pts[k].y * TILE - child.h;             // 站上落点面(原版顶锚 y-48)
409	      child.ai3 = -(e.id + 1);                          // ai[3]=-(碑id+1) 待机锚
410	      child.bInit = true;
411	      if (isDevotee) x[1 + devN++] = child.id + 1;      // localAI[1..2]=信徒 id+1
412	      else if (arcN === 0) { e.ai0 = child.id + 1; arcN = 1; }   // ai[0]=弓手0 id+1
413	      else { e.ai1 = child.id + 1; arcN = 2; }                   // ai[1]=弓手1 id+1
414	    }
415	    if (arcN < 2 || devN < 2) { e.dead = true; return; }   // 生成不足 → 消散
416	    return;
417	  }
418	  // ---- 信徒受击 → 弓手激活(:38826-38839 localAI[0]==1) ----
419	  if (x[0] === 1) {
420	    x[0] = 2;
421	    for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
422	      const dev = enemies(game).find((n) => n.id === x[k + 1] - 1 && n.vanillaId === 438);
423	      if (dev) dev.ai1 = 1;   // :38831 信徒 ai[1]=1(1.4.5.6 无消费方,存档语义保留)
424	      const arcId = k === 0 ? e.ai0 : e.ai1;
425	      const arc = enemies(game).find((n) => n.id === Math.floor(arcId) - 1 && n.vanillaId === 379);
426	      if (arc) arc.ai3 = 0;   // :38834 弓手解除待机(零伤恢复见 cultistArcherWake)
427	    }
428	  }
429	  // ---- 四教徒全灭 → 召 439(:38841-38879) ----
430	  if (e.ai0 !== -1) {
431	    const gone = [0, 1].every((k) => {
432	      const dev = enemies(game).find((n) => n.id === x[k + 1] - 1);
433	      const arc = enemies(game).find((n) => n.id === Math.floor(k === 0 ? e.ai0 : e.ai1) - 1);
434	      return (!dev || dev.dead || dev.vanillaId !== 438)
435	        && (!arc || arc.dead || arc.vanillaId !== 379);
436	    });
437	    if (gone) {
438	      // :38853-38868 侧面 11 格找落地列(±随机向),自碑心向下扫首个实心面
439	      const side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
440	      const tx = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE) + 11 * side;
441	      let ty = Math.floor(e.cy / TILE);
442	      for (let j = -5; j < 12; j++) {
443	        const y = Math.floor(e.cy / TILE) + j;
444	        if (solidTileAt(game, tx, y) && !solidRect(game, tx - 1, tx + 1, y - 3, y - 1)) { ty = y; break; }
445	        if (j === 11) ty = y;
446	      }
447	      const cultist = spawnPart(game, e, 439);
448	      // TabletDestroyed(delay=43200 重置)由 Game 侧 ai0==-1 边沿捕获记账
449	      // （_cultistTabletDone WeakSet 去重）——勿在此再调,避免双路径
450	      if (cultist) {
451	        cultist.x = tx * TILE + 10 - cultist.w / 2;
452	        cultist.y = ty * TILE - cultist.h;              // 原版顶锚 y=tileY*16-2,本仓落底对齐
453	        cultist.facing = Math.sign(e.cx - (tx * TILE + 10)) || 1;
454	        cultist.bInit = true;
455	        e.ai2 = cultist.id;                             // :38875 ai[2]=邪教徒 id
456	      }
457	      e.ai0 = -1; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
458	      // CultistRitual.TabletDestroyed()(复活延迟账)——事件延迟系统未引入,略
459	    }
460	  }
461	  // ---- 仪式倒计时(:38881-38954):300t 满 → 爆散;120t 起每 10t 一枚 526(粒子代) ----
462	  if (e.ai0 === -1) {
463	    e.ai3 += 1;
464	    if (e.ai3 > 300) {
465	      e.dead = true;
466	      for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {                     // :38893-38903 六枚直飞
467	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#B080FF', 3, 3, { life: 30 });
468	      }
469	      for (let k = 0; k < 20; k++) {                    // :38905-38919 二十枚上抛(1/2 保留)
470	        if (Math.random() < 0.5) continue;
471	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#B080FF', 3, 3, { life: 30 });
472	      }
473	    } else if (e.ai3 % 10 === 1 && e.ai3 > 120) {       // :38921-38931
474	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#B080FF', 2, 2.4, { life: 34 });
475	    }
476	  }
477	  // 石碑静止(noGravity,noTileCollide;SetDefaults :14115 段)
478	}
